3287	      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.ChestTrapped'));
3288	    }
3289	    this.openChest?.(chest as never);
3290	    this.input.rightDown = false;

3301	  /** 压力板 135:踩上瞬间 HitSwitch(Collision.SwitchTiles 玩家语义;敌人触发见 Enemy 侧) */
3302	  private checkPressurePlates(): void {
3303	    // 原版 Collision.SwitchTiles(Player.cs:18439):扫玩家 AABB 覆盖的全部格,
3304	    // 命中压板 135 边沿触发(踩上/离开各一次,plateLatch 按格去重)
3305	    const p = this.player;
3306	    const x0 = Math.floor(p.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((p.x + p.w - 1) / TILE);
3307	    const y0 = Math.floor(p.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((p.y + p.h - 1) / TILE);
3308	    // 复用 Set(2026-08 审计 G9:此前每 tick new Set + [...spread],60Hz 恒定分配)
3309	    const pressed = this._platePressed; pressed.clear();
3310	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
3311	      for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
3312	        if (this.wiring?.sheetOf(tx, ty) === 135) {
3313	          const key = tx + ',' + ty;
3314	          pressed.add(key);
3315	          if (!this.plateLatch.has(key)) this.wiring?.hitSwitch(tx, ty);
3316	        }
3317	      }
3318	    }
3319	    for (const key of pressed) this.plateLatch.add(key);
3320	    // Set 迭代中删除当前元素是安全的——省去 [...spread] 复制
3321	    for (const key of this.plateLatch) {
3322	      if (!pressed.has(key)) this.plateLatch.delete(key);
3323	    }
3324	  }

3328	  private updateTriggerTiles(): void {
3329	    const w = this.wiring;
3330	    if (!w) return;
3331	    const st = this.world.store;
3332	    const p = this.player;
3333	    // 测重板:玩家 AABB 覆盖格(原版 PressurePlateHelper 16×10 底部判定)
3334	    const standing = this._weightedStanding; standing.clear();
3335	    const px0 = Math.floor(p.x / TILE), px1 = Math.floor((p.x + p.w - 1) / TILE);
3336	    const py0 = Math.floor(p.y / TILE), py1 = Math.floor((p.y + p.h - 1) / TILE);
3337	    for (const idx of this.triggerTiles428) {
3338	      const x = idx % st.w, y = (idx / st.w) | 0;
3339	      if (x >= px0 && x <= px1 && y >= py0 && y <= py1) standing.add(idx);
3340	    }
3341	    for (const idx of standing) {
3342	      if (!this.weightedLatch.has(idx)) {
3343	        this.weightedLatch.add(idx);
3344	        w.blockPlayerTeleportationForOneIteration = true; // 原版 MoveInto(:135-142)
3345	        w.hitSwitch(idx % st.w, (idx / st.w) | 0);
3346	      }
3347	    }
3348	    for (const idx of this.weightedLatch) {
3349	      if (!standing.has(idx)) {
3350	        this.weightedLatch.delete(idx);
3351	        w.blockPlayerTeleportationForOneIteration = true; // 原版 MoveAwayFrom 双触发
3352	        w.hitSwitch(idx % st.w, (idx / st.w) | 0);
3353	      }
3354	    }
3355	    // 感应器 423:玩家上方(frameY 0=昼 36=夜 72=玩家出入上方,placeStyle 0-2 → frameY/18%3)
3356	    const ptx = Math.floor(p.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor((p.y + p.h) / TILE);
3357	    for (const idx of this.triggerTiles423) {
3358	      const x = idx % st.w, y = (idx / st.w) | 0;
3359	      const mode = Math.floor(st.frameY[idx] / 18) % 3;
3360	      if (mode === 2 && x === ptx && pty > y && pty - y <= 8) {
3361	        w.hitSwitch(x, y);
3362	      }
3363	      // 昼/夜感应由 onDayNight 回调驱动(见 triggerDayNightSensors)
3364	    }
3365	  }

3367	  /** 昼/夜切换时触发全部对应感应器(原版逻辑在 Projectile.LogicSensor 驱动) */
3368	  triggerDayNightSensors() {

3474	  private updateWeather(): void {
3475	    const w = this.world;
3476	    const weather = w.weather;
3477	    const pty = Math.floor(this.player.cy / TILE);
3478	    const gl = w.groundLevel;
3479	    const rl = Math.max(gl + 1, w.rockLevel);
3480	    // atmo 精确公式（Main.cs:62622：(y/16-(65+10×(maxTilesX/4200)²))/(worldSurface/5)，y=视口中心）；
3481	    // 旧版深度线性近似已废弃
3482	    const cam = this.camera;
3483	    const viewCY = cam ? cam.y : this.player.cy;
3484	    const atmoBase = (viewCY / 16 - (65 + 10 * (w.w / 4200) ** 2)) / Math.max(1, gl / 5);
3485	    const atmo = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, atmoBase));
3486	    // 墓园强度逼近（SceneState.cs:415-418 MoveTowards 0.02/0.1）
3487	    const gyTarget = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (this.scene.graveyardTileCount - 16) / (36 - 16)));
3488	    const gyStep = this.graveyardIntensity < gyTarget ? 0.02 : 0.1;

3490	    // 白天 tick 进度（原版 time 0-54000 ↔ 我们 timeOfDay 白天前半段 0.25-0.75 线性）
3491	    const dayTimeTicks = w.clock.isDay ? ((w.clock.timeOfDay - 0.25) / 0.5) * 54000 : 0;
3492	    weather.setWorldSize(w.w, w.h);
3493	    weather.update({
3494	      dayRate: 1,
3495	      dayTime: w.clock.isDay,
3496	      dayTimeTicks,
3497	      playerStrongEnough: this.player.maxHp >= 120,
3498	      playerReadyForKingSlime: this.player.maxHp > 140 && this.player.defense > 8, // Main.cs:64645
3499	      expertMode: false,
3500	      atmo,
3501	      aboveRockLayer: pty < rl,
3502	      graveyardIntensity: this.graveyardIntensity,
3503	      hardMode: !!w.flags.hardMode,
3504	      noOtherEvent: !w.clock.bloodMoon && this.world.invasionType === 0,
3505	      downedSlimeKing: !!w.flags.downedSlimeKing,
3506	      maxTilesX: w.w,
3507	      worldSurface: gl,
3508	      viewCenterY: viewCY,
3509	      onCoinRain: (x, y, type, stack) => {
3510	        // 非实心 32×32 门（WorldGen.cs:71847）
3511	        const st = w.store;
3512	        const tx0 = Math.floor(x / 16), ty0 = Math.floor(y / 16);
3513	        let solid = false;
3514	        for (let dx = 0; dx <= 1 && !solid; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy <= 1; dy++) {
3515	          if (st.inBounds(tx0 + dx, ty0 + dy) && st.isSolid(tx0 + dx, ty0 + dy)) { solid = true; break; }
3516	        }
3517	        if (solid) return;
3518	        const key = type === 71 ? 'coin_silver' : type === 72 ? 'coin_gold' : 'coin_platinum';
3519	        this.spawnDrop(x, y, key, stack, 0, 3);
3520	      },
3521	      onThunder: (distPx) => {
3522	        // 原版 SoundID 43：thunderDistance×15px 处发声——按距离衰减近似

25	export const TOOL_BLUE = 4;
26	export const TOOL_YELLOW = 8;
27	export const TOOL_ACTUATOR = 16;
28	export const TOOL_CUTTER = 32;
29	
30	const MAX_PUMP = 20;   // Wiring.cs:34(MaxPump=20,填充上限 19)
31	const MAX_MECH = 1000; // Wiring.cs:41(MaxMech=1000,注册上限 999)
32	
33	export class Wiring {
34	  readonly st: TileStore;
35	  /** 器件表(P3 注入) */
36	  device: DeviceHandler | null = null;
37	  /** 传送器对执行(引擎侧搬迁玩家/敌人;P3 注入) */
38	  teleportPair: ((a: [number, number], b: [number, number]) => void) | null = null;
39	  /** 世界地表行(蜥蜴砖致动限制 Wiring.cs:2375) */
40	  worldSurface = 0;
41	  /** 世世纪杀花已击败(同上) */
42	  planteraDowned = false;
43	
44	  running = false;                                  // :23
45	  blockPlayerTeleportationForOneIteration = false;  // :22(测重板抑制传送)
46	  private currentWireColor = 0;                     // :46(1红 2蓝 3绿 4黄;彩线灯泡用)
47	
48	  // BFS 状态(:24-27)。点用 idx(y*w+x) 编码。
49	  private wireSkip = new Set<number>();
50	  private wireList: number[] = [];      // FIFO(head 指针)
51	  private wireDir: number[] = [];       // 与 wireList 平行的进入方向 0下 1上 2右 3左
52	  private toProcess = new Map<number, number>(); // :27 分线盒再访预算
53	
54	  // 逻辑门(:28-32)
55	  private gatesCurrent: number[] = [];
56	  private gatesNext: number[] = [];
57	  private gatesDone = new Map<number, boolean>();
58	  private lampsToCheck: number[] = [];
59	  private pixelBoxTriggers = new Map<number, number>();
60	
61	  // 传送器对(:33;px 坐标,-1 = 未捕获)与泵(:34-40)
62	  private tp: Array<[number, number]> = [[-1, -1], [-1, -1]];
63	  private inPumpX = new Int16Array(MAX_PUMP); private inPumpY = new Int16Array(MAX_PUMP);
64	  private outPumpX = new Int16Array(MAX_PUMP); private outPumpY = new Int16Array(MAX_PUMP);
65	  private numInPump = 0; private numOutPump = 0;
66	
67	  // 冷却注册(:41-45)
68	  private mechX = new Int32Array(MAX_MECH + 1);
69	  private mechY = new Int32Array(MAX_MECH + 1);
70	  private mechTime = new Int32Array(MAX_MECH + 1);
71	  private numMechs = 0;
72	
73	  constructor(st: TileStore) {
74	    this.st = st;
75	  }
76	
77	  private p2i(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.st.w + x; }
78	  private i2x(i: number): number { return i % this.st.w; }
79	  private i2y(i: number): number { return (i / this.st.w) | 0; }
80	  private sheetAt(x: number, y: number): number { return this.sheetOf(x, y); }
81	  /** 该格的原版 tile id(sheet;非 vanilla tile 返回 -1)。器件表按 sheet 分发 */
82	  sheetOf(x: number, y: number): number {
83	    if (!this.st.inBounds(x, y)) return -1;
84	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.st.type[this.p2i(x, y)]];
85	    return d?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
86	  }
87	  /** 多格器件整体去重(devices.ts 用) */
88	  skipCell(x: number, y: number): void { this.skipWire(x, y); }
89	
90	  // ============ UpdateMech(Wiring.cs:88-161)每 tick 调 ============
91	
92	  updateMech(): void {
93	    for (let i = this.numMechs - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
94	      this.mechTime[i]--;
95	      const mx = this.mechX[i], my = this.mechY[i];
96	      const sh = this.sheetAt(mx, my);
97	      const raw = this.st.type[this.p2i(mx, my)] !== 0;
98	      if (raw && sh === 144) {
99	        const ti = this.p2i(mx, my);
100	        if (this.st.frameY[ti] === 0) {
101	          this.mechTime[i] = 0;
102	        } else {
103	          // frameX/18 → 周期帧数(:102-120)
104	          let period = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 18);
105	          period = period === 0 ? 60 : period === 1 ? 180 : period === 2 ? 300 : period === 3 ? 30 : period === 4 ? 15 : 60;
106	          // IEEERemainder(t, period) === 0(注意负数:IEEERemainder 结果范围 (-p,p])
107	          const r = this.mechTime[i] - period * Math.round(this.mechTime[i] / period);
108	          if (r === 0) {
109	            this.mechTime[i] = 18000;
110	            this.tripWire(mx, my, 1, 1);
111	          }
112	        }
113	      }
114	      if (this.mechTime[i] <= 0) {
115	        if (raw && sh === 144) {
116	          const ti = this.p2i(mx, my);
117	          this.st.setTile(mx, my, this.st.type[ti], this.st.frameX[ti], 0);
118	        }
119	        if (raw && sh === 411) {
120	          // 引爆器 2×2 帧复位(:135-151)
121	          const ti = this.p2i(mx, my);
122	          const num1 = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] % 36 / 18);
123	          const num2 = Math.floor(this.st.frameY[ti] % 36 / 18);
124	          const tileX = mx - num1, tileY = my - num2;
125	          let num3 = 36;
126	          if (this.st.frameX[this.p2i(tileX, tileY)] >= 36) num3 = -36;
127	          for (let ix = tileX; ix < tileX + 2; ix++) {
128	            for (let iy = tileY; iy < tileY + 2; iy++) {
129	              const ii = this.p2i(ix, iy);
130	              if (this.sheetAt(ix, iy) === 411) this.st.setTile(ix, iy, this.st.type[ii], this.st.frameX[ii] + num3, this.st.frameY[ii]);
131	            }
132	          }
133	        }
134	        // 数组下移移除(:152-158)
135	        for (let k = i; k < this.numMechs; k++) {
136	          this.mechX[k] = this.mechX[k + 1];
137	          this.mechY[k] = this.mechY[k + 1];
138	          this.mechTime[k] = this.mechTime[k + 1];
139	        }
140	        this.numMechs--;
141	      }
142	    }
143	  }
144	
145	  // ============ CheckMech(Wiring.cs:313-327)============
146	
147	  checkMech(i: number, j: number, time: number): boolean {
148	    for (let k = 0; k < this.numMechs; k++) {
149	      if (this.mechX[k] === i && this.mechY[k] === j) return false;
150	    }
151	    if (this.numMechs >= 999) return false;
152	    this.mechX[this.numMechs] = i;
153	    this.mechY[this.numMechs] = j;
154	    this.mechTime[this.numMechs] = time;
155	    this.numMechs++;
156	    return true;
157	  }
158	
159	  // ============ HitSwitch(Wiring.cs:163-252)============
160	
161	  hitSwitch(i: number, j: number): void {
162	    if (!this.st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
163	    const sh = this.sheetAt(i, j);
164	    const ti = this.p2i(i, j);
165	    // 压板/轨道/感应器/测重板/垫板/高尔夫(:167-171)与宝石锁(:172-176):直接 Trip
166	    if (sh === 135 || sh === 314 || sh === 423 || sh === 428 || sh === 442 || sh === 476) {
167	      this.tripWire(i, j, 1, 1);
168	      return;
169	    }
170	    if (sh === 440) { // 宝石锁 3×3
171	      this.tripWire(i, j, 3, 3);
172	      return;
173	    }
174	    if (sh === 136) { // 开关 frameY 0↔18(:177-182)
175	      this.st.setTile(i, j, this.st.type[ti], this.st.frameX[ti], this.st.frameY[ti] !== 0 ? 0 : 18);
176	      this.tripWire(i, j, 1, 1);
177	      return;
178	    }
179	    if (sh === 443) { // 热喷泉(:183-184)
180	      this.device?.(this, 443, i, j);
181	      return;
182	    }
183	    if (sh === 144) { // 计时器开/关(:185-196)
184	      if (this.st.frameY[ti] === 0) {
185	        this.st.setTile(i, j, this.st.type[ti], this.st.frameX[ti], 18);
186	        this.checkMech(i, j, 18000);
187	      } else {
188	        this.st.setTile(i, j, this.st.type[ti], this.st.frameX[ti], 0);
189	      }
190	      return;
191	    }
192	    if (sh === 441 || sh === 468) { // 陷阱箱 2×2(:197-208)
193	      const num1 = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 18) * -1;
194	      const num2 = Math.floor(this.st.frameY[ti] / 18) * -1;
195	      let num3 = num1 % 4;
196	      if (num3 < -1) num3 += 2;
197	      this.tripWire(num3 + i, num2 + j, 2, 2);
198	      return;
199	    }
200	    if (sh === 467) { // 水晶陷阱箱(frameX/36==4 才触发,:209-222)
201	      if (Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 36) !== 4) return;
202	      const num1 = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 18) * -1;
203	      const num2 = Math.floor(this.st.frameY[ti] / 18) * -1;
204	      let num3 = num1 % 4;
205	      if (num3 < -1) num3 += 2;
206	      this.tripWire(num3 + i, num2 + j, 2, 2);
207	      return;
208	    }
209	    if (sh === 132 || sh === 411) { // 拉杆/引爆器 2×2 翻帧(:223-251)
210	      let delta = 36;
211	      const num2 = Math.floor(this.st.frameX[ti] / 18) * -1;
212	      const num3 = Math.floor(this.st.frameY[ti] / 18) * -1;
213	      let num4 = num2 % 4;
214	      if (num4 < -1) { num4 += 2; delta = -36; }
215	      const ax = num4 + i, ay = num3 + j;
216	      if (this.sheetAt(ax, ay) === 411) this.checkMech(ax, ay, 60);
217	      for (let ix = ax; ix < ax + 2; ix++) {
218	        for (let iy = ay; iy < ay + 2; iy++) {
219	          if (!this.st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
220	          const ii = this.p2i(ix, iy);
221	          const s2 = this.sheetAt(ix, iy);
222	          if (s2 === 132 || s2 === 411) {
223	            this.st.setTile(ix, iy, this.st.type[ii], this.st.frameX[ii] + delta, this.st.frameY[ii]);
224	          }
225	        }
226	      }
227	      this.tripWire(ax, ay, 2, 2);
228	      return;
229	    }
230	  }
231	
232	  // ============ TripWire(Wiring.cs:379-529)============
233	
234	  tripWire(left: number, top: number, width: number, height: number): void {
235	    this.running = true;
236	    this.wireList.length = 0;
237	    this.wireDir.length = 0;
238	    const st = this.st;
239	    const pairs: Array<[[number, number], [number, number]]> = [];
240	    const bits = [TileStore.WIRE_RED, TileStore.WIRE_BLUE, TileStore.WIRE_GREEN, TileStore.WIRE_YELLOW];
241	    // 四色 pass 顺序固定 红(1)蓝(2)绿(3)黄(4)(:405-508);每色前重置传送器对与泵
242	    for (let color = 1; color <= 4; color++) {
243	      this.tp[0] = [-1, -1]; this.tp[1] = [-1, -1];
244	      this.wireList.length = 0;
245	      const bit = bits[color - 1];
246	      for (let x = left; x < left + width; x++) {
247	        for (let y = top; y < top + height; y++) {
248	          if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
249	          if (st.wire[this.p2i(x, y)] & bit) this.wireList.push(this.p2i(x, y));
250	        }
251	      }
252	      if (this.wireList.length > 0) {
253	        this.numInPump = 0;
254	        this.numOutPump = 0;
255	        this.hitWire(color);
256	        if (this.numInPump > 0 && this.numOutPump > 0) this.xferWater();
257	      }
258	      if (this.tp[0][0] >= 0 && this.tp[1][0] >= 0) pairs.push([[...this.tp[0]], [...this.tp[1]] as [number, number]]);
259	    }
260	    this.running = false;
261	    // 四色收集完的传送器对统一执行(:520-526)
262	    for (const [a, b] of pairs) this.teleportPair?.(a, b);
263	    this.pixelBoxPass();
264	    this.logicGatePass();
265	  }
266	
267	  // ============ HitWire BFS(Wiring.cs:693-827)============
268	
269	  private hitWire(wireType: number): void {
270	    const st = this.st;
271	    // 种子:SkipWire + 预算 4 + 方向 0(:695-703)
272	    this.wireDir.length = 0;
273	    const n = this.wireList.length;
274	    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
275	      const p = this.wireList[k];
276	      this.skipWireI(p);
277	      this.toProcess.set(p, 4);
278	      this.wireDir.push(0);
279	    }
280	    this.currentWireColor = wireType;
281	    let head = 0;
282	    while (head < this.wireList.length) {
283	      const key = this.wireList[head];
284	      const num1 = this.wireDir[head];
285	      head++;
286	      const x = this.i2x(key), y = this.i2y(key);
287	      if (!this.wireSkip.has(key)) this.hitWireSingle(x, y);
288	      // 邻居顺序:0下 1上 2右 3左(:713-739)
289	      for (let index1 = 0; index1 < 4; index1++) {
290	        const X = index1 === 0 ? x : index1 === 1 ? x : index1 === 2 ? x + 1 : x - 1;
291	        const Y = index1 === 0 ? y + 1 : index1 === 1 ? y - 1 : y;
292	        if (X < 2 || X >= st.w - 2 || Y < 2 || Y >= st.h - 2) continue; // :740
293	        const curSheet = this.sheetAt(x, y);
294	        const nbSheet = this.sheetAt(X, Y);
295	        // :748-750 邻居是分线盒/像素盒 → 不给预算
296	        let num2 = 3;
297	        if (nbSheet === 424 || nbSheet === 445) num2 = 0;
298	        // :751-768 分线盒方向门控(frameX/18 三态)
299	        if (curSheet === 424) {
300	          const mode = Math.floor(st.frameX[this.p2i(x, y)] / 18);
301	          if (mode === 0) { if (index1 === num1) continue; }
302	          else if (mode === 1) {
303	            if (!((num1 === 0 && index1 === 3) || (num1 === 3 && index1 === 0) || (num1 === 1 && index1 === 2) || (num1 === 2 && index1 === 1))) continue;
304	          } else if (mode === 2) {
305	            if (!((num1 === 0 && index1 === 2) || (num1 === 2 && index1 === 0) || (num1 === 1 && index1 === 3) || (num1 === 3 && index1 === 1))) continue;
306	          }
307	        }
308	        // :769-780 像素盒仅同向直通,并记录触发值(垂直 2 / 水平 1)
309	        if (curSheet === 445) {
310	          if (index1 === num1) {
311	            const v = (index1 === 0 || index1 === 1) ? 2 : 1;
312	            this.pixelBoxTriggers.set(key, (this.pixelBoxTriggers.get(key) ?? 0) | v);
313	          } else continue;
314	        }
315	        // :781-799 邻居须带当前色
316	        const bit = wireType === 1 ? TileStore.WIRE_RED : wireType === 2 ? TileStore.WIRE_BLUE : wireType === 3 ? TileStore.WIRE_GREEN : TileStore.WIRE_YELLOW;
317	        if (!(st.wire[this.p2i(X, Y)] & bit)) continue;
318	        // :800-819 预算消耗或入队
319	        const nb = this.p2i(X, Y);
320	        const budget = this.toProcess.get(nb);
321	        if (budget !== undefined) {
322	          const left2 = budget - 1;
323	          if (left2 === 0) this.toProcess.delete(nb);
324	          else this.toProcess.set(nb, left2);
325	        } else {
326	          this.wireList.push(nb);
327	          this.wireDir.push(index1);
328	          if (num2 > 0) this.toProcess.set(nb, num2);
329	        }
330	      }
331	    }
332	    this.wireSkip.clear();
333	    this.toProcess.clear();
334	  }
335	
336	  // ============ HitWireSingle 核心 case(Wiring.cs:829+)============
337	
338	  private hitWireSingle(x: number, y: number): void {
339	    const st = this.st;
340	    const i = this.p2i(x, y);
341	    const type = st.type[i];
342	    const sheet = this.sheetAt(x, y);
343	    // :833-834 先致动(active 检查之前,空格上的致动线也会翻)
344	    if (st.wire[i] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATOR) this.actuateForced(x, y);
345	    // :835 raw active = type≠0(致动态也继续)
346	    if (type === 0) return;
347	    // 核心 case(牵扯 Wiring 内部状态),其余全表委托 devices.ts
348	    switch (sheet) {
349	      case 144: // 计时器:信号 = 打开并重置计时(:839-843)
350	        this.hitSwitch(x, y);
351	        return;
352	      case 419: { // 逻辑门灯:翻转 + SkipWire + 入队(:1075-1086)
353	        let d = 18;
354	        if (st.frameX[i] >= 18) d = -18;
355	        if (st.frameX[i] === 36) d = 0;
356	        this.skipWire(x, y);
357	        st.setTile(x, y, type, st.frameX[i] + d, st.frameY[i]);
358	        this.lampsToCheck.push(i);
359	        return;
360	      }
361	      case 235: { // 传送器成对捕获(:1251-1271)
362	        const tx = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18);
363	        if (st.wall[i] === 87 && y > this.worldSurface && !this.planteraDowned) return;
364	        const halfOff = st.half[i] ? 0.5 : 0;
365	        if (this.tp[0][0] === -1) { this.tp[0] = [tx, y + halfOff]; return; }
366	        if (this.tp[0][0] === tx && this.tp[0][1] === y + halfOff) return;
367	        this.tp[1] = [tx, y + halfOff];
368	        return;
369	      }
370	      case 142: case 143: { // 入水/出水泵 2×2 四格收集(:1793-1863)
371	        const py = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 18);
372	        let px = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18);
373	        if (px > 1) px -= 2;
374	        const ox = x - px;
375	        this.skipWire(ox, py); this.skipWire(ox, py + 1);
376	        this.skipWire(ox + 1, py); this.skipWire(ox + 1, py + 1);
377	        const cells: Array<[number, number]> = [[ox, py + 1], [ox + 1, py + 1], [ox, py], [ox + 1, py]];
378	        if (sheet === 142) {
379	          for (let k = 0; k < 4 && this.numInPump < 19; k++) {
380	            this.inPumpX[this.numInPump] = cells[k][0];
381	            this.inPumpY[this.numInPump] = cells[k][1];
382	            this.numInPump++;
383	          }
384	        } else {
385	          for (let k = 0; k < 4 && this.numOutPump < 19; k++) {
386	            this.outPumpX[this.numOutPump] = cells[k][0];
387	            this.outPumpY[this.numOutPump] = cells[k][1];
388	            this.numOutPump++;
389	          }
390	        }
391	        return;
392	      }
393	      case 429: { // 彩线灯泡:按当前色翻对应位(:1325-1357)
394	        const q = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18);
395	        let num53 = 0, on = false;
396	        switch (this.currentWireColor) {
397	          case 1: num53 = 18; on = !(q % 2 >= 1); break;
398	          case 2: num53 = 72; on = !(q % 8 >= 4); break;
399	          case 3: num53 = 36; on = !(q % 4 >= 2); break;
400	          case 4: num53 = 144; on = !(q % 16 >= 8); break;
401	        }
402	        st.setTile(x, y, type, st.frameX[i] + (on ? num53 : -num53), st.frameY[i]);
403	        return;
404	      }
405	    }
406	    // 致动后不再吃器件信号(原版各 case 里 actuator 时早退的近似统一处理;
407	    // 门/传送带/宝石块在原版各自判断,其余器件致动时信号照常——按原版逐 case 已在 devices 内)
408	    this.device?.(this, sheet, x, y);
409	  }
410	
411	  // ============ XferWater(Wiring.cs:329-377)============
412	
413	  private xferWater(): void {
414	    const st = this.st;
415	    for (let a = 0; a < this.numInPump; a++) {
416	      const i1 = this.inPumpX[a], j1 = this.inPumpY[a];
417	      if (!st.inBounds(i1, j1)) continue;
418	      const ii = st.idx(i1, j1);
419	      let liquid1 = st.liquid[ii];
420	      if (liquid1 > 0) {
421	        const lava = st.liquidType[ii] === 2, honey = st.liquidType[ii] === 3;
422	        for (let b = 0; b < this.numOutPump; b++) {
423	          const i2 = this.outPumpX[b], j2 = this.outPumpY[b];
424	          if (!st.inBounds(i2, j2)) continue;
425	          const oi = st.idx(i2, j2);
426	          let liquid2 = st.liquid[oi];
427	          if (liquid2 < 255) {
428	            let flag1 = st.liquidType[oi] === 2, flag2 = st.liquidType[oi] === 3;
429	            if (liquid2 === 0) { flag1 = lava; flag2 = honey; }
430	            if (lava === flag1 && honey === flag2) {
431	              let move = liquid1;
432	              if (move + liquid2 > 255) move = 255 - liquid2;
433	              st.setLiquid(i2, j2, liquid2 + move, lava ? 2 : honey ? 3 : 1);
434	              st.setLiquid(i1, j1, liquid1 - move, st.liquidType[ii] || 1);
435	              liquid1 = st.liquid[ii];
436	              if (st.liquid[ii] === 0) break;
437	            }
438	          }
439	        }
440	      }
441	    }
442	  }
443	
444	  // ============ PixelBoxPass(Wiring.cs:531-553)============
445	
446	  private pixelBoxPass(): void {
447	    const st = this.st;
448	    for (const [key, v] of this.pixelBoxTriggers) {
449	      const x = this.i2x(key), y = this.i2y(key);
450	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
451	      const i = this.p2i(x, y);
452	      if (v === 1) {
453	        if (st.frameX[i] !== 0) st.setTile(x, y, st.type[i], 0, st.frameY[i]);
454	      } else if (v === 3) {
455	        if (st.frameX[i] !== 18) st.setTile(x, y, st.type[i], 18, st.frameY[i]);
456	      }
457	    }
458	    this.pixelBoxTriggers.clear();
459	  }
460	
461	  // ============ LogicGatePass / CheckLogicGate(Wiring.cs:555-691)============
462	
463	  private logicGatePass(): void {
464	    if (this.gatesCurrent.length !== 0) return; // 已在级联中
465	    this.gatesDone.clear();
466	    let lampHead = 0, gateHead = 0;
467	    while (this.lampsToCheck.length > lampHead) {
468	      while (this.lampsToCheck.length > lampHead) {
469	        const lamp = this.lampsToCheck[lampHead++];
470	        this.checkLogicGate(this.i2x(lamp), this.i2y(lamp));
471	      }
472	      // _GatesNext → _GatesCurrent,逐个 TripWire(递归;_GatesDone 防环)(:567-585)
473	      const next = this.gatesNext;
474	      this.gatesNext = [];
475	      this.gatesCurrent = next;
476	      gateHead = 0;
477	      while (this.gatesCurrent.length > gateHead) {
478	        const key = this.gatesCurrent[gateHead];
479	        gateHead++;
480	        if (this.gatesDone.get(key)) continue;
481	        this.gatesDone.set(key, true);
482	        this.tripWire(this.i2x(key), this.i2y(key), 1, 1);
483	      }
484	      this.gatesCurrent.length = 0; // 原版 Queue Dequeue 排空;残留会让下次级联被早退守卫吞掉
485	    }
486	    this.lampsToCheck.length = 0;
487	    this.gatesDone.clear();
488	    if (this.blockPlayerTeleportationForOneIteration) {
489	      this.blockPlayerTeleportationForOneIteration = false;
490	    }
491	  }
492	
493	  /** 触发逻辑门(压板等直接 Poke,P4/P6 用;Wiring.cs:254-260 的单机语义) */
494	  pokeLogicGate(lampX: number, lampY: number): void {
495	    this.lampsToCheck.push(this.p2i(lampX, lampY));
496	    this.logicGatePass();
497	  }
498	
499	  private checkLogicGate(lampX: number, lampY: number): void {
500	    const st = this.st;
501	    if (!st.inBounds(lampX, lampY)) return;
502	    for (let y = lampY; y < st.h; y++) {
503	      if (!st.inBounds(lampX, y)) break;
504	      const i = this.p2i(lampX, y);
505	      if (st.type[i] === 0) break;
506	      const sh = this.sheetAt(lampX, y);
507	      if (sh === 420) {
508	        const wasDone = this.gatesDone.get(i) ?? false;
509	        const num1 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 18); // 门种类 0-5
510	        const flag2 = st.frameX[i] === 18;  // 当前输出 ON
511	        const flag3 = st.frameX[i] === 36;  // 故障输出态
512	        if (num1 < 0) break;
513	        // 向上数灯(:614-629)
514	        let num2 = 0, num3 = 0;
515	        let faulty = false;
516	        for (let y2 = y - 1; y2 > 0; y2--) {
517	          const j = this.p2i(lampX, y2);
518	          if (st.type[j] !== 0 && this.sheetAt(lampX, y2) === 419) {
519	            if (st.frameX[j] === 36) { faulty = true; break; }
520	            num2++;
521	            if (st.frameX[j] === 18) num3++;
522	          } else break;
523	        }
524	        let out: boolean;
525	        switch (num1) { // :631-653
526	          case 0: out = num2 === num3; break;   // AND
527	          case 1: out = num3 > 0; break;        // OR
528	          case 2: out = num2 !== num3; break;   // XOR
529	          case 3: out = num3 === 0; break;      // NOR
530	          case 4: out = num3 === 1; break;      // NAND
531	          case 5: out = num3 !== 1; break;      // XNOR
532	          default: return;
533	        }
534	        const flag6 = !faulty && flag3;                 // 故障存在且门当前 off → 强制不发
535	        let flag7 = false;                              // 故障灯本轮被翻 → 概率通过
536	        if (faulty && st.frameX[this.p2i(lampX, lampY)] === 36) flag7 = true;
537	        // :658 无变化则不动作
538	        if (!((out !== flag2) || flag6 || flag7)) break;
539	        st.setTile(lampX, y, st.type[i], out ? 18 : 0, st.frameY[i]);
540	        if (faulty) st.setTile(lampX, y, st.type[i], 36, st.frameY[i]);
541	        this.skipWire(lampX, y);
542	        let fire: boolean = !faulty || flag7;
543	        if (flag7 && num2 > 0) fire = Math.random() < num3 / num2;
544	        if (flag6) fire = false;
545	        if (!fire) break;
546	        if (!wasDone) {
547	          this.gatesNext.push(i);
548	          break;
549	        }
550	        // 重复输出 = 冒烟反馈(Wiring.cs:683-685;烟雾粒子由 devices 侧近似,此处仅返回)
551	        break;
552	      }
553	      if (sh !== 419) break;
554	    }
555	  }
556	
557	  // ============ 致动器(Wiring.cs:262-280, 2373-2405)============
558	
559	  actuate(i: number, j: number): boolean {
560	    const st = this.st;
561	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return false;
562	    if (!(st.wire[this.p2i(i, j)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATOR)) return false;
563	    if (st.isActuated(i, j)) this.reActive(i, j);
564	    else this.deActive(i, j);
565	    return true;
566	  }
567	
568	  actuateForced(i: number, j: number): void {
569	    if (this.st.isActuated(i, j)) this.reActive(i, j);
570	    else this.deActive(i, j);
571	  }
572	
573	  /** 缩入(:2373-2396):蜥蜴砖(地表下未杀花)/宝箱顶限制 */
574	  private deActive(i: number, j: number): void {
575	    const st = this.st;
576	    if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
577	    const t = this.p2i(i, j);
578	    if (st.type[t] === 0) return;
579	    if (this.sheetAt(i, j) === 226 && j > this.worldSurface && !this.planteraDowned) return;
580	    const def = TILE_DEFS[st.type[t]];
581	    let solid = !!def?.solid;
582	    const sh = this.sheetAt(i, j);
583	    if (sh === 314 || sh === 386 || sh === 387 || sh === 388 || sh === 389 || sh === 476) solid = false;
584	    // 上方是宝箱/祭坛则拒绝(:2389,CanKillTile 近似:实心即可杀)
585	    if (!solid) return;
586	    if (st.inBounds(i, j - 1) && st.type[this.p2i(i, j - 1)] !== 0) {
587	      const above = this.sheetAt(i, j - 1);
588	      if (above === 21 || above === 88 || above === 467 || above === 237) return; // 宝箱族/祭坛
589	    }
590	    st.setActuated(i, j, true);
591	  }
592	
593	  private reActive(i: number, j: number): void {
594	    this.st.setActuated(i, j, false);
595	  }
596	
597	  // ============ SkipWire(Wiring.cs:78-86)============
598	
599	  private skipWire(x: number, y: number): void { this.wireSkip.add(this.p2i(x, y)); }
600	  private skipWireI(i: number): void { this.wireSkip.add(i); }
601	
602	  // ============ 导线放置/移除(WorldGen.cs:34219-34327 语义)============
603	
604	  /** 放置某色线;已存在返回 false */
605	  placeWire(x: number, y: number, colorBit: number): boolean {
606	    const st = this.st;
607	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
608	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
609	    if (st.wire[i] & colorBit) return false;
610	    st.setWire(x, y, st.wire[i] | colorBit);
611	    return true;
612	  }
613	
614	  /** 移除某色线;不存在返回 false */
615	  killWire(x: number, y: number, colorBit: number): boolean {
616	    const st = this.st;
617	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
618	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
619	    if (!(st.wire[i] & colorBit)) return false;
620	    st.setWire(x, y, st.wire[i] & ~colorBit);
621	    return true;
622	  }
623	
624	  placeActuator(x: number, y: number): boolean {
625	    const st = this.st;
626	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
627	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
628	    if (st.wire[i] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATOR) return false;
629	    st.setWire(x, y, st.wire[i] | TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATOR);
630	    return true;
631	  }
632	
633	  killActuator(x: number, y: number): boolean {
634	    const st = this.st;
635	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
636	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
637	    if (!(st.wire[i] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATOR)) return false;
638	    st.setWire(x, y, st.wire[i] & ~TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATOR);
639	    return true;
640	  }
641	
642	  // ============ MassWireOperation(Wiring.cs:2407-2558)============
643	
644	  /**
645	   * 宏伟蓝图/五彩扳手 L 形批量操作。
646	   * mode: TOOL_* 位组合;consume 回调返回剩余可用数(false=耗尽),
647	   * 返回实际消耗 {wire, actuator}。
648	   */
649	  massWireOperation(
650	    ps: [number, number], pe: [number, number], mode: number,
651	    wires: number, actuators: number,
652	  ): { wire: number; actuator: number; aborted: boolean } {
653	    const dir = true; // 原版 dir = player.direction==1;先垂直段。false 时先水平
654	    let wireLeft = wires, actuatorLeft = actuators;
655	    let aborted = false;
656	    const step = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
657	      // 返回 false = 材料耗尽中止
658	      if (!this.st.inBounds(x, y)) return true;
659	      const i = this.st.idx(x, y);
660	      if (!(mode & TOOL_CUTTER)) {
661	        const order: Array<[number, number]> = [
662	          [TOOL_RED, TileStore.WIRE_RED], [TOOL_GREEN, TileStore.WIRE_GREEN],
663	          [TOOL_BLUE, TileStore.WIRE_BLUE], [TOOL_YELLOW, TileStore.WIRE_YELLOW],
664	        ];
665	        for (const [m, bit] of order) {
666	          if (mode & m && !(this.st.wire[i] & bit)) {
667	            if (wireLeft <= 0) return false;
668	            wireLeft--;
669	            this.placeWire(x, y, bit);
670	          }
671	        }
672	        if (mode & TOOL_ACTUATOR && !(this.st.wire[i] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATOR)) {
673	          if (actuatorLeft <= 0) return false;
674	          actuatorLeft--;
675	          this.placeActuator(x, y);
676	        }
677	      } else {
678	        if (mode & TOOL_RED) this.killWire(x, y, TileStore.WIRE_RED);
679	        if (mode & TOOL_GREEN) this.killWire(x, y, TileStore.WIRE_GREEN);
680	        if (mode & TOOL_BLUE) this.killWire(x, y, TileStore.WIRE_BLUE);
681	        if (mode & TOOL_YELLOW) this.killWire(x, y, TileStore.WIRE_YELLOW);
682	        if (mode & TOOL_ACTUATOR) this.killActuator(x, y);
683	      }
684	      return true;
685	    };
686	    // 第一段:主轴(dir=true → 垂直)(:2428-2454)
687	    const sx = Math.sign(pe[0] - ps[0]), sy = Math.sign(pe[1] - ps[1]);
688	    let px = ps[0], py = ps[1];
689	    const first = dir ? sy : sx;
690	    {
691	      const from = dir ? ps[1] : ps[0];
692	      const to = dir ? pe[1] : pe[0];
693	      for (let v = from; v !== to && !aborted; v += first) {
694	        if (dir) py = v; else px = v;
695	        if (!step(px, py)) { aborted = true; break; }
696	      }
697	    }
698	    // 第二段:副轴(:2455-2484)
699	    {
700	      if (dir) py = pe[1]; else px = pe[0];
701	      const from = dir ? ps[0] : ps[1];
702	      const to = dir ? pe[0] : pe[1];
703	      const stepDir = dir ? sx : sy;
704	      for (let v = from; v !== to && !aborted; v += stepDir) {
705	        if (dir) px = v; else py = v;
706	        if (!step(px, py)) { aborted = true; break; }
707	      }
708	    }
709	    // 端点(:2485-2486)
710	    if (!aborted) step(pe[0], pe[1]);
711	    return { wire: wires - wireLeft, actuator: actuators - actuatorLeft, aborted };
712	  }
713	}
714

44	function swapTile(w: Wiring, x: number, y: number, fromKey: string, toKey: string): void {
45	  const st = w.st;

48	  const cur = st.type[i];
49	  const from = TILE_BY_KEY[fromKey], to = TILE_BY_KEY[toKey];
50	  if (cur === from) st.setTile(x, y, to, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
51	  else if (cur === to) st.setTile(x, y, from, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
52	}

56	function flipBlock(
57	  w: Wiring, x: number, y: number,
58	  blockW: number, blockH: number, tilesW: number, tilesH: number,
59	  axis: 'x' | 'y', threshold: number, delta: number,
60	): void {
61	  const st = w.st;
62	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
63	  const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % blockW / 18);
64	  const oy = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % blockH / 18);
65	  let d = delta;
66	  const ai = st.idx(ox, oy);
67	  if (axis === 'y' ? st.frameY[ai] >= threshold : st.frameX[ai] >= threshold) d = -delta;
68	  for (let ix = ox; ix < ox + tilesW; ix++) {
69	    for (let iy = oy; iy < oy + tilesH; iy++) {
70	      if (!st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
71	      const ii = st.idx(ix, iy);
72	      w.skipCell(ix, iy);
73	      if (axis === 'y') st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii], st.frameY[ii] + d);
74	      else st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii] + d, st.frameY[ii]);
75	    }
76	  }
77	}
78	
79	/** 单格帧翻转(火把/蜡烛等) */
80	function flipCell(w: Wiring, x: number, y: number, delta: number): void {
81	  const st = w.st;
82	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
83	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
84	  let d = delta;
85	  if (st.frameX[i] >= delta) d = -delta;
86	  st.setTile(x, y, st.type[i], st.frameX[i] + d, st.frameY[i]);
87	}
88	
89	/** 陷阱(137)发射(Wiring.cs:1495-1743 + Item.cs placeStyle)。frameY/18=种类:
90	 *  0飞镖(98) 1超级飞镖(184) 2烈焰(187,隐形+尘埃) 3尖刺球(185) 4长矛(186) */
91	function fireTrap(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): void {
92	  const st = w.st;
93	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
94	  const kind = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 18);
95	  if (kind === 0 || kind === 1 || kind === 2) {
96	    if (!w.checkMech(x, y, 200)) return;
97	    // 方向:frameX 0=左 18=右;frameY<36 平置 / ≥36 竖置(上下)
98	    const dirX = st.frameX[i] === 0 ? -1 : st.frameX[i] === 18 ? 1 : 0;
99	    const dirY = st.frameX[i] < 36 ? 0 : st.frameX[i] < 72 ? -1 : 1;
100	    const speed = kind === 0 ? 12 : kind === 1 ? 12 : 5;
101	    const dmg = kind === 0 ? 20 : 40;
102	    const px = x * 16 + 8 + 10 * dirX, py = y * 16 + 8 + 10 * dirY;
103	    ctx.shootProjectile(px, py, dirX * speed, dirY * speed, dmg, kind === 0 ? 'dart' : kind === 1 ? 'superdart' : 'flame');
104	  } else if (kind === 3) {
105	    // 尖刺球:CheckMech 300 + 同场数量惩罚表(Wiring.cs:1536-1599)
106	    if (!w.checkMech(x, y, 300)) return;
107	    let budget = 200;
108	    const near = ctx.countProjectiles('spiky', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8, 1200);
109	    for (let k = 0; k < near; k++) {
110	      budget -= k < 1 ? 50 : k < 2 ? 15 : k < 4 ? 10 : k < 7 ? 8 : k < 12 ? 6 : k < 20 ? 5 : k < 38 ? 4 : k < 65 ? 3 : k < 105 ? 2 : 1;
111	    }
112	    if (budget <= 0) return;
113	    let dx = 0, dy = 0;
114	    switch (Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18)) {
115	      case 0: case 1: dy = 1; break;
116	      case 2: dy = -1; break;
117	      case 3: dx = -1; break;
118	      case 4: dx = 1; break;
119	    }
120	    const px = x * 16 + 8 + 14 * dx, py = y * 16 + 8 + 14 * dy;
121	    ctx.shootProjectile(px, py, dx * 4, dy * 4, 40, 'spiky');
122	  } else if (kind === 4) {
123	    // 下向火焰(CheckMech 90)
124	    if (!w.checkMech(x, y, 90)) return;
125	    let dx = 0, dy = 0;
126	    switch (Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18)) {
127	      case 0: dx = -1; break;
128	      case 1: dx = 1; break;
129	      case 2: dy = -1; break;
130	      case 3: dy = 1; break;
131	    }
132	    ctx.shootProjectile(x * 16 + 8 + 10 * dx, y * 16 + 8 + 10 * dy, dx * 8, dy * 8, 60, 'spear');
133	  }
134	}
135	
136	/** 热喷泉 443(Wiring.cs:2284-2312;由 HitSwitch 直调) */
137	export function geyserTrap(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): boolean {
138	  const st = w.st;
139	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
140	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
141	  const num = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 36);
142	  const i1 = x - Math.floor((st.frameX[i] - num * 36) / 18);
143	  if (!w.checkMech(i1, y, 200)) return false;
144	  const up = num < 2;
145	  ctx.shootProjectile(
146	    (i1 + 1) * 16, (y + (up ? 0 : 1)) * 16,
147	    0, up ? -8 : 8, 20, 'geyser',
148	  );
149	  return true;
150	}
151	
152	/** 雕像 style → 原版 NPC id 表(Wiring.cs:1892-1996)。
153	 *  num79 = frameX/36 + (frameY/54 % 3) * 55 */
154	const STATUE_NPC: Record<number, number | number[]> = {
155	  5: 73, 13: 24, 30: 6, 35: 2,
156	  51: [299, 538], 52: 356, 53: 357, 54: [355, 358], 55: [367, 366],
157	  56: [359, 359, 359, 359, 360], 57: 377, 58: 300, 59: [364, 362], 60: 148, 61: 361,
158	  62: [487, 486, 485], 64: 86, 65: 490, 66: 82, 67: 449, 68: 167, 69: 480, 70: 48,
159	  71: [170, 180, 171], 72: 481, 73: 482, 74: 430, 75: 489, 76: 611, 77: 602,
160	  78: [595, 596, 599, 597, 600, 598], 79: [616, 617],
161	  80: [671, 672], 81: 673, 82: [674, 675], // 1.4.4+ 新雕像（:2260-2267）
162	};
163	/** flag7（生成区实心 → poof 不生成）的 style（:2270-2282；64=小丑? 71=史莱姆王组） */
164	const STATUE_SOLID_CHECK = new Set([64, 71]);
165	/** 单独分支的雕像 style（Wiring.cs :2290-2440）。dx/dy 为生成点偏移、
166	 *  solid=生成区实心时 poof、pool=随机池（28 蛇三色等） */
167	const STATUE_NPC_DIRECT: Record<number, { npc: number | number[]; dx?: number; dy?: number; solid?: boolean }> = {
168	  4: { npc: 1, dy: -12 },
169	  7: { npc: 49, dx: -4, dy: -6 },
170	  8: { npc: 55, dy: -12 },
171	  9: { npc: 46, dy: -12 },
172	  10: { npc: 21 },
173	  16: { npc: 42, dy: -12, solid: true },
174	  18: { npc: 67, dy: -12 },
175	  23: { npc: 63, dy: -12 },
176	  27: { npc: 85, dx: -9 },
177	  28: { npc: [74, 297, 298], dy: -12 },
178	  42: { npc: 58, dy: -12 },
179	  50: { npc: 65, solid: true }, // 史莱姆王雕像（:2385-2399）
180	};
181	/** 物品雕像 style → [原版 item id, ...MechSpawn 配额同查的等价 id]
182	 *  （2 星 184/1735/1868、17 炸弹 166、37 心 58/1734/1867；:2408-2420） */
183	const STATUE_ITEM: Record<number, { item: number; mechIds: number[] }> = {
184	  2: { item: 184, mechIds: [184, 1735, 1868] },
185	  17: { item: 166, mechIds: [166] },
186	  37: { item: 58, mechIds: [58, 1734, 1867] },
187	};
188	
189	function pick<T>(arr: T[]): T { return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)]; }
190	
191	/** 雕像 105(Wiring.cs:1866-2215) */
192	function fireStatue(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): void {
193	  const st = w.st;
194	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
195	  // 锚点与 style(:1867-1879)
196	  const num73 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18);
197	  let num74 = 0;
198	  let rem = num73;
199	  while (rem >= 2) { rem -= 2; num74++; }
200	  const ox = x - rem;
201	  const ax = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 36 / 18);
202	  const ay = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 54 / 18);
203	  const num79 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 36) + Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 54 % 3) * 55;
204	  w.skipCell(ax, ay); w.skipCell(ax, ay + 1); w.skipCell(ax, ay + 2);
205	  w.skipCell(ax + 1, ay); w.skipCell(ax + 1, ay + 1); w.skipCell(ax + 1, ay + 2);
206	  const px = ax * 16 + 16, py = (ay + 3) * 16;
207	  // 生成区实心判定（:2274 SolidTiles(num139-2..num139+3, num138..num138+2)；flag7/部分直刷用）
208	  const spawnAreaSolid = () => {
209	    for (let sx = ax - 2; sx <= ax + 3; sx++) {
210	      for (let sy = ay; sy <= ay + 2; sy++) {
211	        if (st.inBounds(sx, sy) && st.isSolid(sx, sy)) return true;
212	      }
213	    }
214	    return false;
215	  };
216	  // NPC 雕像主表（spawn 于雕像底中心 (px,py)；:2268-2282）
217	  const entry = STATUE_NPC[num79];
218	  if (entry !== undefined) {
219	    const npcId = Array.isArray(entry) ? pick(entry) : entry;
220	    if (w.checkMech(ax, ay, 30) && ctx.mechSpawnOk(px, py, npcId)) {
221	      if (!STATUE_SOLID_CHECK.has(num79) || !spawnAreaSolid()) {
222	        ctx.spawnNpc(npcId, px, py);
223	      } else {
224	        ctx.sfx('tink', px, py);
225	      }
226	    }
227	    return;
228	  }
229	  // 单独分支 NPC 雕像（各自偏移/实心判定；:2290-2440）
230	  const direct = STATUE_NPC_DIRECT[num79];
231	  if (direct !== undefined) {
232	    const npcId = Array.isArray(direct.npc) ? pick(direct.npc) : direct.npc;
233	    if (w.checkMech(ax, ay, 30) && ctx.mechSpawnOk(px, py, npcId)) {
234	      if (!direct.solid || !spawnAreaSolid()) {
235	        ctx.spawnNpc(npcId, px + (direct.dx ?? 0), py + (direct.dy ?? 0));
236	      } else {
237	        ctx.sfx('tink', px, py);
238	      }
239	    }
240	    return;
241	  }
242	  // 物品雕像（CheckMech 600；心/星要过全部等价 id 的 MechSpawn 配额；:2408-2420）
243	  const itemEntry = STATUE_ITEM[num79];
244	  if (itemEntry !== undefined) {
245	    const ok = w.checkMech(ax, ay, 600)
246	      && itemEntry.mechIds.every((mid) => ctx.itemSpawnOk(px, py, mid));
247	    if (ok) ctx.dropItem(itemEntry.item, px, py - 16);
248	    return;
249	  }
250	  switch (num79) {
251	    case 34: { // 蘑菇雕像:2×3 变 tile 349 动画(:2108-2119)
252	      const T349 = TILE_BY_KEY['v_349_mushroom_statue'] ?? 0;
253	      for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
254	        for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
255	          st.setTile(ax + dx, ay + dy, T349, dx * 18 + 216, dy * 18);
256	        }
257	      }
258	      return;
259	    }
260	    case 40: case 41: // 城镇 NPC 搬迁(CheckMech 300,:2133-2186)
261	      if (w.checkMech(ax, ay, 300)) ctx.relocateTownNpc(num79 === 40 ? 0 : 1, px, py);
262	      return;
263	    case 63: // 随机传送雕像：164 + 额外 MechSpawn(165)（:2226-2229）
264	      if (w.checkMech(ax, ay, 30) && ctx.mechSpawnOk(px, py, 164) && ctx.mechSpawnOk(px, py, 165)) {
265	        ctx.spawnNpc(164, px, py);
266	      }
267	      return;
268	  }
269	}
270	
271	/** 巨石雕像 531(Wiring.cs:1747-1762) */
272	function fireBoulderStatue(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, x: number, y: number): void {
273	  const st = w.st;
274	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
275	  const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 36 / 18);
276	  const oy = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 36 / 18);
277	  if (!w.checkMech(ox, oy, 900)) return;
278	  w.skipCell(ox, oy); w.skipCell(ox + 1, oy); w.skipCell(ox, oy + 1); w.skipCell(ox + 1, oy + 1);
279	  ctx.shootProjectile(ox * 16 + 16, (oy + 1) * 16, 0, 0, 70, 'boulder');
280	}
281	
282	/** 可接线灯器件的开关帧判定(Wiring.cs 翻帧 delta 推导):on = frame%(2δ) < δ。
283	 *  火把 δ=66(22px×3 变体一组)、蜡烛族 δ=18、2×2 灯具 δ=36、吊灯/彩灯 δ=54、篝火走 Y 轴 δ=36 */
284	export const LIGHT_TOGGLE: Record<number, ['x' | 'y', number]> = {
285	  4: ['x', 66],
286	  33: ['x', 18], 174: ['x', 18], 49: ['x', 18], 372: ['x', 18],
287	  42: ['x', 18], 92: ['x', 18], 93: ['x', 18],
288	  95: ['x', 36], 100: ['x', 36], 126: ['x', 36], 173: ['x', 36], 564: ['x', 36],
289	  34: ['x', 54], 149: ['x', 54],
290	  215: ['y', 36],
291	};
292	
293	/** 该格灯光是否点亮(非灯器件恒 true;供 LightingEngine 查询) */
294	export function lightIsOn(sheet: number, frameX: number, frameY: number): boolean {
295	  const t = LIGHT_TOGGLE[sheet];
296	  if (!t) return true;
297	  const v = t[0] === 'x' ? frameX : frameY;
298	  return v % (2 * t[1]) < t[1];
299	}
300	
301	/** HitWireSingle 器件主分发表。返回 true = 已处理 */
302	export function deviceTable(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, sheet: number, x: number, y: number): boolean {
303	  const st = w.st;
304	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
305	  switch (sheet) {
306	    case 4: // 火把 frameX ±66(:1207-1213)
307	      flipCell(w, x, y, 66);
308	      return true;
309	    case 10: // 关门 → 开(随机方向+反向,原版 :1171-1188)
310	      ctx.doorOpen(x, y);
311	      return true;
312	    case 11:
313	      ctx.doorClose(x, y);
314	      return true;
315	    case 15: { // 马桶(frameY/40 判定,:1194-1204;喷水近似粒子)
316	      if (Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 1 && Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 20) return true;
317	      if (w.checkMech(x, y, 60)) ctx.sfx('splash', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
318	      return true;
319	    }
320	    case 33: case 174: case 49: case 372: // 蜡烛族 frameX ±18(:1472-1480)
321	      flipCell(w, x, y, 18);
322	      return true;
323	    case 34: // 吊灯 3×3 frameX ±54(:1405-1428)
324	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 108, 54, 3, 3, 'x', 54, 54);
325	      return true;
326	    case 35: case 139: case 207: case 410: case 480: case 509:
327	      // 南瓜灯/八音盒/喷泉/三色天塔柱:原版 SwitchMB/SwitchFountain/SwitchMonolith。
328	      // 引擎暂无对应状态系统 → toast 占位
329	      ctx.toast('装置切换(音乐/喷泉/天塔柱系统未接入)');
330	      return true;
331	    case 42: // 挂链灯笼 1×2 frameX ±18(:1214-1227)
332	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 36, 36, 1, 2, 'x', 18, 18);
333	      return true;
334	    case 92: // 路灯柱 1×6 frameX ±18(:1483-1494)
335	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 18, 108, 1, 6, 'x', 18, 18);
336	      return true;
337	    case 93: // 蒂基火炬 1×3 frameX ±18(:1228-1243)
338	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 18, 54, 1, 3, 'x', 18, 18);
339	      return true;
340	    case 95: case 100: case 126: case 173: case 564: // 2×2 灯具族 frameX ±36(:1381-1404)
341	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 72, 36, 2, 2, 'x', 36, 36);
342	      return true;
343	    case 105:
344	      fireStatue(w, ctx, x, y);
345	      return true;
346	    case 130: { // 通电石:上方有宝箱则不缩(:878-884)
347	      const above = y > 0 ? w.sheetOf(x, y - 1) : -1;
348	      if (above === 21 || above === 88 || above === 467) return true;
349	      swapTile(w, x, y, 'v_130_active_stone_block', 'v_131_inactivestoneblock');
350	      return true;
351	    }
352	    case 131:
353	      swapTile(w, x, y, 'v_130_active_stone_block', 'v_131_inactivestoneblock');
354	      return true;
355	    case 137:
356	      fireTrap(w, ctx, x, y);
357	      return true;
358	    case 141: // 炸药:清格+爆炸(:1781-1785)
359	      w.skipCell(x, y);
360	      st.setTile(x, y, 0);
361	      ctx.explode(x, y, 500);
362	      return true;
363	    case 149: // 节日彩灯 frameX ±54(:1244-1250)
364	      flipCell(w, x, y, 54);
365	      return true;
366	    case 209: { // 大炮（Wiring.cs:1237-1346 1:1）：旋转+开火复合状态机。
367	      // num31=块内列(0/3=旋转态,1/2=待发) num32=行内偏移 num35=仰角带(frameY/54,0-8)
368	      // num36=水平带(frameX/72: 0/1=右向 大炮/兔兔炮, 3/4=左向)
369	      const num31 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 72 / 18);
370	      const num32 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 54 / 18);
371	      const ox = x - num31, oy = y - num32;
372	      const num35 = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 54);
373	      const num36 = Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 72);
374	      let num37 = -1;
375	      if (num31 === 1 || num31 === 2) num37 = num32;
376	      // 旋转半边：列3 → 仰角 -54（带0停）；列0 → +54（带8停）
377	      let num38 = 0;
378	      if (num31 === 3) num38 = -54;
379	      if (num31 === 0) num38 = 54;
380	      if (num35 >= 8 && num38 > 0) num38 = 0;
381	      if (num35 === 0 && num38 < 0) num38 = 0;
382	      if (num38 !== 0) {
383	        for (let cx = ox; cx < ox + 4; cx++) {
384	          for (let cy = oy; cy < oy + 3; cy++) {
385	            if (!st.inBounds(cx, cy)) continue;
386	            w.skipCell(cx, cy);
387	            const ci = st.idx(cx, cy);
388	            st.setTile(cx, cy, st.type[ci], st.frameX[ci], st.frameY[ci] + num38);
389	          }
390	        }
391	      }
392	      // 水平朝向带翻转：3/4 带 + 行0/1 → frameX ±72（下一次触发反向）
393	      if ((num36 === 3 || num36 === 4) && (num37 === 0 || num37 === 1)) {
394	        const fx = num36 === 3 ? 72 : -72;
395	        for (let cx = ox; cx < ox + 4; cx++) {
396	          for (let cy = oy; cy < oy + 3; cy++) {
397	            if (!st.inBounds(cx, cy)) continue;
398	            w.skipCell(cx, cy);
399	            const ci = st.idx(cx, cy);
400	            st.setTile(cx, cy, st.type[ci], st.frameX[ci] + fx, st.frameY[ci]);
401	          }
402	        }
403	      }
404	      if (num37 === -1) return true; // 只旋转未开火
405	      // 开火半边：半仰角(<2)时左向(带3/4)不发射（flag2）
406	      const flag2 = !((num36 === 3 || num36 === 4) && num37 < 2);
407	      const damage = num36 === 0 ? 300 : num36 === 1 ? 350 : 40; // 兔兔炮350/普通300
408	      const time = num36 === 0 ? 480 : num36 === 1 ? 3600 : 30;
409	      if (!w.checkMech(ox, oy, time) || !flag2) return true;
410	      // ShootFromCannon(x, y, 仰角带, direction=num36+1)：方向1/2右 3/4左；弹药未接入以巨石近似
411	      const dirR = num36 <= 1 ? 1 : -1;
412	      const ang = (num35 * Math.PI) / 16; // 仰角带 0-8 ≈ 0-90°
413	      ctx.shootProjectile(ox * 16 + 24, oy * 16, dirR * 7 * Math.cos(ang), -7 * Math.sin(ang) - 2, damage, 'boulder');
414	      return true;
415	    }
416	    case 210: // 地雷:原版 ExplodeMine(:1786-1788)清格+爆炸
417	      st.setTile(x, y, 0);
418	      ctx.explode(x, y, 100);
419	      return true;
420	    case 212: { // 雪球发射器:CheckMech 10 发射(:946-1002)
421	      const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 54 / 18);
422	      const oy = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % 54 / 18);
423	      if (!w.checkMech(x, y, 10)) return true;
424	      ctx.shootProjectile(ox * 16 + 16, oy * 16, 8, -8, 20, 'boulder');
425	      return true;
426	    }
427	    case 215: // 篝火 3×2 frameY ±36(:1003-1020)
428	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 54, 36, 3, 2, 'y', 36, 36);
429	      return true;
430	    case 216: // 烟花火箭(:1189-1192;近似粒子升空)
431	      w.skipCell(x, y);
432	      ctx.sfx('roar', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
433	      return true;
434	    case 244: // 泡泡机 3×2 frameX ±54(:1272-1293)
435	      flipBlock(w, x, y, 54, 36, 3, 2, 'x', 54, 54);
436	      return true;
437	    case 255: case 256: case 257: case 258: case 259: case 260: case 261: { // 宝石块 off→on(:863-873)
438	      if (st.wire[i] & 16) return true; // 原版有致动器时不响应
439	      const to = TILE_BY_KEY[GEMSPARK_ON[sheet - 255]] ?? 0;
440	      if (to) st.setTile(x, y, to, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
441	      return true;
442	    }
443	    case 262: case 263: case 264: case 265: case 266: case 267: case 268: { // on→off
444	      if (st.wire[i] & 16) return true;
445	      const to = TILE_BY_KEY[GEMSPARK_OFF[sheet - 262]] ?? 0;
446	      if (to) st.setTile(x, y, to, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
447	      return true;
448	    }
449	    case 314: // 压板轨道:CheckMech 5 + FlipSwitchTrack(:1429-1433;矿车系统占位)
450	      w.checkMech(x, y, 5);
451	      return true;
452	    case 335: // 烟花盒 2×2(:1294-1304)
453	      w.skipCell(x, y); w.skipCell(x + 1, y);
454	      if (w.checkMech(x, y, 30)) ctx.sfx('roar', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
455	      return true;
456	    case 338: // 烟花喷泉 1×2(:1305-1324)
457	      w.skipCell(x, y);
458	      if (w.checkMech(x, y, 30)) ctx.sfx('roar', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
459	      return true;
460	    case 386: case 387: { // 活板门开/关互换(ShiftTrapdoor;帧近似)
461	      const openK = 'v_386_trapdooropen', closedK = 'v_387_trap_door_closed';
462	      const toOpen = sheet === 387;
463	      const target = TILE_BY_KEY[toOpen ? openK : closedK] ?? 0;
464	      if (!target) return true;
465	      const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % 36 / 18);
466	      for (let ix = ox; ix < ox + 2; ix++) {
467	        if (!st.inBounds(ix, y)) continue;
468	        const ii = st.idx(ix, y);
469	        w.skipCell(ix, y);

67	/** Buff 名:原版 BuffName.<Internal>(Lang 未就绪回退中文兜底) */
68	export function buffName(t: BuffType): string {
69	  return Lang.buffName(BUFF_DEFS[t].vanillaBuff) ?? BUFF_DEFS[t].fallbackName;
70	}
71	
72	/** Buff 描述:自有键(机制数值是本仓库实现,不用原版通用描述) */
73	export function buffDesc(t: BuffType): string {
74	  return Lang.text(BUFF_DEFS[t].descKey);
75	}
76	
77	/** HP/s 汇总表（UpdateBuffs lifeRegen 项换算：lifeRegen N = N/2 HP/s） */
78	const HP_PER_SEC: Partial<Record<BuffType, number>> = {
79	  [BuffType.Regen]: 2,   // lifeRegen+4
80	  [BuffType.Honey]: 1,   // lifeRegen+2
81	};
82	
83	export class BuffState {
84	  active = new Map<BuffType, number>(); // 剩余秒（≈buffTime/60）
85	  campfireAccum = 0;
86	  lanternAccum = 0;
87	  /** 当前篝火光环强度(Game 每 20tick 扫描写入:篝火+1,心灯额外+1) */
88	  campfireHeal = 1;
89	  /** 心灯(lifeRegen+2 = 1 HP/s,Player.cs:12247):Game 扫描写入 0/1 */
90	  lanternHeal = 0;
91	  /** AddBuff 语义（Player.cs:5138-5150）：同类取 max 不缩短——浸入类 buff 可每帧无脑续期 */
92	  apply(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
93	    const cur = this.active.get(type);
94	    if (cur === undefined || cur < durationSec) this.active.set(type, durationSec);
95	  }
96	  has(type: BuffType): boolean {
97	    return this.active.has(type);
98	  }
99	  remove(type: BuffType) {
100	    this.active.delete(type);
101	  }
102	  /** 每逻辑帧（dt 秒）。返回本帧生命净变化（正=回复,负=持续伤害/DoT,无视无敌帧与防御） */
103	  tick(dt: number): number {
104	    let heal = 0;
105	    for (const [t, time] of [...this.active]) {
106	      const left = time - dt;
107	      if (left <= 0) this.active.delete(t);
108	      else this.active.set(t, left);
109	    }
110	    // 定额 HP/s buff（Regeneration 2 HP/s / Honey 1 HP/s）
111	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Regen, 2, dt);
112	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 1, dt);
113	    // 环境 DoT debuff（Player.cs:18793-18934 lifeRegen 负项 / 2 = HP/s）
114	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.OnFire, -4, dt);        // lifeRegen-8
115	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Burning, -30, dt);      // lifeRegen-60
116	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Suffocation, -20, dt);  // lifeRegen-40
117	    // 蜂蜜对冲（Player.cs:18934-18940:lifeRegen<0 时 honey 额外 lifeRegen+4 = +2 HP/s）——
118	    // DoT 系统已实装,补上 buff-system-port 备忘里标"暂缺"的分支
119	    const dotActive = heal < 0;
120	    if (dotActive && this.active.has(BuffType.Honey)) {
121	      heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 2, dt, true);
122	    }
123	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Campfire)) {
124	      // 原版 lifeRegen+1 = 0.5 HP/s(Player.cs:18990/12244-12245)→ 每 2 秒 1 HP
125	      this.campfireAccum += dt;
126	      if (this.campfireAccum >= 2) {
127	        this.campfireAccum -= 2;
128	        heal += this.campfireHeal;
129	      }
130	    }
131	    if (this.lanternHeal > 0) {
132	      this.lanternAccum += dt;
133	      if (this.lanternAccum >= 1) {
134	        this.lanternAccum -= 1;
135	        heal += this.lanternHeal;
136	      }
137	    }
138	    return heal;
139	  }
140	  /** 定额回复/伤害 buff 的独立累加器（Map 存每 buff 的秒余数;负速率=DoT）。
141	   *  separate=true 用独立累加器键(蜂蜜对冲与常规蜂蜜回复分账,避免共用余数) */
142	  private rateLeft = new Map<string, number>();
143	  private rateAccum(t: BuffType, hpPerSec: number, dt: number, separate = false): number {
144	    if (!this.active.has(t)) { this.rateLeft.delete(`${t}:${separate}`); return 0; }
145	    const key = `${t}:${separate}`;
146	    let left = (this.rateLeft.get(key) ?? 0) + hpPerSec * dt;
147	    let heal = 0;
148	    if (hpPerSec >= 0) {
149	      while (left >= 1) { left -= 1; heal += 1; }
150	    } else {
151	      while (left <= -1) { left += 1; heal -= 1; }
152	    }
153	    this.rateLeft.set(key, left);
154	    return heal;
155	  }
156	  // ---- 属性聚合（幂等读取，不做副作用写入）----
157	  get moveSpeedMult(): number {
158	    let m = this.active.has(BuffType.Agility) ? 1.25 : 1;   // Swiftness moveSpeed += 0.25
159	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Burning)) m *= 0.5;         // Burning moveSpeed *= 0.5（:18886）
160	    return m;
161	  }
162	  get defenseBonus(): number {
163	    return this.active.has(BuffType.Ironskin) ? 8 : 0;     // Ironskin statDefense += 8（1456）
164	  }
165	  get healthBonus(): number {
166	    return this.active.has(BuffType.Resistance) ? 80 : 0;
167	  }
168	  get hasThorns(): boolean {
169	    return this.active.has(BuffType.Thorns);
170	  }
171	  get healBlocked(): boolean {
172	    return this.active.has(BuffType.Resistance);
173	  }
174	  // ---- 药水族效果聚合（Player.cs UpdateBuffs 各 case 1:1）----
175	  /** 黑曜石皮:lavaImmune + fireWalk + 着火免疫（:9573-9577） */
176	  get obsidianSkin(): boolean {
177	    return this.active.has(BuffType.ObsidianSkin);
178	  }
179	  get gills(): boolean {
180	    return this.active.has(BuffType.Gills);
181	  }
182	  get slowFall(): boolean {
183	    return this.active.has(BuffType.Featherfall);
184	  }
185	  get canWaterWalk(): boolean {
186	    return this.active.has(BuffType.WaterWalking);
187	  }
188	  get manaRegenBuff(): boolean {
189	    return this.active.has(BuffType.ManaRegen);
190	  }
191	  /** 魔法伤害 +20%（:9669） */
192	  get magicDmgBonus(): number {
193	    return this.active.has(BuffType.MagicPower) ? 0.2 : 0;
194	  }
195	  /** 箭伤 ×1.1（:9712;本仓库 ranged 管道统一消费,乘 ranged 全系——近义注明） */
196	  get arrowDmgMult(): number {
197	    return this.active.has(BuffType.Archery) ? 1.1 : 1;
198	  }
199	}
200	

```

92	/** 以玩家中心扫描并计算全部 Zone 标志（对应 ScanAndApplyToPlayer 主链） */
93	export function scanScene(
94	  world: World, centerX: number, centerY: number, townNPCCount: number,
95	): SceneFlags {
96	  const st = world.store;
97	  const w = st.w, h = st.h;
98	  const underworldLayer = world.lavaLine || (h - 200); // Main.UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200
99	  const worldSurface = world.groundLevel;
100	  const rockLevel = world.rockLevel;
101	  const tileX = Math.floor(centerX / 16);
102	  const tileY = Math.floor(centerY / 16);
103	
104	  // 169×124 采样窗口（SceneMetrics.ZoneScanSize）
105	  const sx0 = Math.max(0, tileX - 84), sx1 = Math.min(w - 1, tileX + 84);
106	  const sy0 = Math.max(0, tileY - 61), sy1 = Math.min(h - 1, tileY + 62);
107	  const counts = new Map<number, number>();
108	  let sunflower = 0;
109	  let oceanSand = 0;
110	  const oceanTopY = (worldSurface + rockLevel) / 2 + 40; // oceanLevel（WorldGen.cs:4393）
111	  const beachDist = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06)); // beachDistance（WorldGen.cs:4407 近似）
112	  for (let y = sy0; y <= sy1; y++) {
113	    for (let x = sx0; x <= sx1; x++) {
114	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
115	      if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
116	      const t = st.type[i];
117	      counts.set(t, (counts.get(t) ?? 0) + 1);
118	      if (t === SUNFLOWER) sunflower++;
119	      // isDesertBiomeSand && oceanDepths（SceneMetrics L376-380）：海洋沙不计入沙漠沙
120	      if (SAND_TILES.has(t) && y <= oceanTopY && (x < beachDist || x > w - beachDist)) oceanSand++;
121	    }
122	  }
123	
124	  // 聚合（AggregateTileCounts L603-670：含神圣/邪恶/猩红互减、向日葵 -10）
125	  let holy = countSet(counts, HOLY_TILES);
126	  let evil = countSet(counts, EVIL_TILES) + sunflower * -10;
127	  let blood = countSet(counts, BLOOD_TILES) + sunflower * -10;
128	  const holyRaw = holy;
129	  holy -= evil; holy -= blood;
130	  evil -= holyRaw; blood -= holyRaw;
131	  holy = Math.max(0, holy); evil = Math.max(0, evil); blood = Math.max(0, blood);
132	
133	  const jungle = countSet(counts, JUNGLE_TILES);
134	  const snow = countSet(counts, SNOW_TILES);
135	  const mushroom = countSet(counts, MUSHROOM_TILES);
136	  const meteor = countSet(counts, METEOR_TILES);
137	  const dungeonTiles = countSet(counts, DUNGEON_TILES);
138	  // DesertSandTileCount（SceneMetrics L665：sand - oceanSand；oceanSand 在扫描循环内按 oceanDepths 逐格计）
139	  const desert = Math.max(0, countSet(counts, SAND_TILES) - oceanSand);
140	  const graveyard = Math.max(0, countSet(counts, GRAVEYARD_TILES) - (sunflower >> 1));
141	
142	  // Zone 判定（CalculateZones L673-697）
143	  const zoneUnderworldHeight = tileY > underworldLayer;
144	  const belowSurface = tileY > worldSurface;
145	  const centerWall = st.inBounds(tileX, tileY) ? st.wall[st.idx(tileX, tileY)] : 0;
146	  const zoneDungeon = dungeonTiles >= DUNGEON_THRESHOLD && belowSurface && DUNGEON_WALLS.has(centerWall);
147	  const zoneDesert = desert >= DESERT_THRESHOLD;
148	
149	  // oceanDepths（WorldGen.cs:4390-4400：y <= (worldSurface+rockLayer)/2+40 且 x 距边 < beachDistance）
150	  const beachDistance = Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06));
151	  const oceanTop = (worldSurface + rockLevel) / 2 + 40;
152	  const zoneBeach = tileY <= oceanTop && (tileX < beachDistance || tileX > w - beachDistance);
153	
154	  return {
155	    tileX, tileY,
156	    belowSurface,
157	    zoneSkyHeight: tileY <= worldSurface * 0.35,
158	    zoneOverworldHeight: tileY <= worldSurface && tileY > worldSurface * 0.35,
159	    zoneDirtLayerHeight: tileY <= rockLevel && tileY > worldSurface,
160	    zoneRockLayerHeight: tileY <= underworldLayer && tileY > rockLevel,
161	    zoneUnderworldHeight,
162	    zoneCorrupt: evil >= CORRUPTION_THRESHOLD,
163	    zoneCrimson: blood >= CRIMSON_THRESHOLD,
164	    zoneHallow: holy >= HALLOW_THRESHOLD,
165	    zoneJungle: jungle >= JUNGLE_THRESHOLD && !zoneUnderworldHeight,
166	    zoneSnow: snow >= SNOW_THRESHOLD,
167	    zoneDesert,
168	    snowTileCount: snow,
169	    desertSandTileCount: desert, // 已扣海洋沙（oceanDepths 门见上）近似——扫描窗内沙漠区不含海岸时与原版一致
170	    graveyardTileCount: graveyard,
171	    zoneGlowshroom: mushroom >= MUSHROOM_THRESHOLD,
172	    zoneMeteor: meteor >= METEOR_THRESHOLD,
173	    zoneGraveyard: graveyard >= GRAVEYARD_THRESHOLD,
174	    zoneDungeon,
175	    zoneTemple: centerWall === 87,
176	    zoneBeach,
177	    zoneUndergroundDesert: zoneDesert && belowSurface && SANDSTONE_WALLS.has(centerWall),
178	    townNPCCount,
179	  };
180	}
181	

```

1	// 蜂巢破坏语义（原版 WorldGen.KillTile 大 switch，1.4.5.6 WorldGen.cs）：
2	//   case 225 Hive Block（:65524-65545）：Next(3)==0 → 本格变满蜂蜜液体（honey:true,
3	//     liquid=255——打破蜂巢块"流蜜"的出处）；否则掉落 item 1124（Hive）且
4	//     Next(2)==0 时出蜂：Next(3)==0 → 2 只，否则 1 只（type=Next(210,212) 即 210/211），
5	//     初速 vx=vy=Next(-200,201)*0.002。
6	//   case 231 Larva（:53254-53280）：gore 碎裂无掉落；最近玩家与破坏点
7	//     曼哈顿距离 <4800px（300 格）→ NPC.SpawnOnPlayer(222) 蜂后。
8	import type { TileStore } from './TileStore';
9	import type { RNG } from '../core/rng';
10	
11	export interface HiveBreakHooks {
12	  /** 掉落蜂巢物品（原版 Item.NewItem 1124） */
13	  dropHiveItem: (px: number, py: number) => void;
14	  /** 生成蜜蜂（210/211，锚点 x*16+8, y*16+15，初速 ±0.4） */
15	  spawnBee: (px: number, py: number, rng: RNG) => void;
16	}
17	
18	/** 蜂巢块破坏（KillTile case 225）。返回是否变成蜂蜜（true=流蜜，无掉落） */
19	export function breakHiveBlock(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, rng: RNG, hooks: HiveBreakHooks): boolean {
20	  st.setTile(x, y, 0);
21	  if (rng.next() < 1 / 3) {
22	    // 满格蜂蜜。setLiquid 触发 onLiquidChanged → LiquidSim 唤醒，蜜会流动
23	    st.setLiquid(x, y, 255, 3);
24	    return true;
25	  }
26	  hooks.dropHiveItem(x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
27	  if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
28	    const n = rng.next() < 1 / 3 ? 2 : 1;
29	    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) hooks.spawnBee(x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 15, rng);
30	  }
31	  return false;
32	}
33	
34	/** 幼虫破坏距离判定（KillTile case 231）：最近玩家曼哈顿距离 <4800px 才召蜂后 */
35	export function larvaSummonInRange(playerX: number, playerY: number, tileX: number, tileY: number): boolean {

44	export function canTileHurt(t: number, fireWalk: boolean): boolean {
45	  if (t === tid(230)) return false; // 仅 For-the-Worthy 种子,本作不支持
46	  if (TOUCH_IMMEDIATE.has(t) || TOUCH_BLEEDING.has(t) || SUFFOCATE.has(t)) return true;

52	
53	/**
54	 * Collision.LavaCollision / WetCollision（Collision.cs:1575-1690）盒判定:
55	 * 实体包围盒覆盖到任一液体格（liquid>0 且类型匹配）即命中。
56	 * liquidType: 1 水 / 2 岩浆 / 3 蜂蜜。
57	 */
58	export function liquidCollision(st: TileStore, px: number, py: number, pw: number, ph: number, liquidType: number): boolean {
59	  const TS = 16;
60	  const i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px / TS));
61	  const i1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor((px + pw - 0.01) / TS));
62	  const j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py / TS));
63	  const j1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor((py + ph - 0.01) / TS));
64	  for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
65	    for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) {
66	      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
67	      if (st.liquid[idx] > 0 && st.liquidType[idx] === liquidType) return true;
68	    }
69	  }
70	  return false;
71	}
72	
73	/**
74	 * Collision.StickyTiles（Collision.cs:3375）:盒内蛛网(51)/活木(229,无坡)命中。
75	 * 返回命中格（供 StickyMovement 挣断销毁）;未命中返回 null。
76	 */
77	export function stickyTiles(st: TileStore, px: number, py: number, pw: number, ph: number): HurtTile | null {
78	  const TS = 16;
79	  const bySheet = BY_SHEET;
80	  const WEB = bySheet.get(51) ?? -1;
81	  const LIVING = bySheet.get(229) ?? -1;
82	  const i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px / TS) - 1);
83	  const i1 = Math.min(st.w, Math.floor((px + pw) / TS) + 2);
84	  const j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py / TS) - 1);
85	  const j1 = Math.min(st.h - 40, Math.floor((py + ph) / TS) + 2);
86	  for (let i = i0; i < i1; i++) {
87	    for (let j = j0; j < j1; j++) {
88	      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
89	      if (!st.flags[idx]) continue;
90	      const t = st.type[idx];
91	      if (t !== WEB && !(t === LIVING && st.slope[idx] === 0)) continue;
92	      if (px + pw > i * TS && px < i * TS + TS && py + ph > j * TS && py < j * TS + 16.01) {
93	        return { type: t, x: i, y: j };
94	      }
95	    }
96	  }
97	  return null;
98	}
99	
100	/**
101	 * HurtTiles（Collision.cs:3128）:实体包围盒外扩 1 tile 扫描,返回首个可伤块。
102	 * 半砖下移 8px;窒息族缩盒 2px(num6);坡面块走原版 num7 双对面命中判定
103	 * （仅坡上薄侧接触时跳过——本作坡面数据少,按原版比较式移植）。
104	 */
105	export function hurtTiles(
106	  st: TileStore, px: number, py: number, pw: number, ph: number, fireWalk: boolean,
107	): HurtTile | null {
108	  const TS = 16;
109	  const i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px / TS) - 1);
110	  const i1 = Math.min(st.w, Math.floor((px + pw) / TS) + 2);
111	  const j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py / TS) - 1);
112	  const j1 = Math.min(st.h - 40, Math.floor((py + ph) / TS) + 2);
113	  for (let i = i0; i < i1; i++) {
114	    for (let j = j0; j < j1; j++) {
115	      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
116	      if (!st.flags[idx]) continue;
117	      const t = st.type[idx];
118	      if (!canTileHurt(t, fireWalk)) continue;
119	      let ty = j * TS;
120	      let th = TS;
121	      if (st.half[idx]) { ty += 8; th -= 8; }
122	      const num6 = SUFFOCATE.has(t) ? 2 : 0;
123	      // 盒重叠（原版四向比较）
124	      if (px + pw - num6 < i * TS || px + num6 > i * TS + TS
125	        || py + ph - num6 < ty - 0.5 || py + num6 > ty + th + 0.5) continue;
126	      // 坡面:实体须与坡的"厚"侧重叠（num7==2 = 全在薄侧 → 跳过）
127	      const slope = st.slope[idx];
128	      if (slope > 0) {
129	        if (num6 > 0) continue; // 窒息族不与坡面交互（原版同款）
130	        let num7 = 0;
131	        if ((slope === 1 || slope === 4) && px > i * TS) num7++;          // 右高
132	        if ((slope === 2 || slope === 3) && px + pw < i * TS + TS) num7++; // 左高
133	        if ((slope === 3 || slope === 4) && py > ty) num7++;               // 下高(倒坡)
134	        if ((slope === 1 || slope === 2) && py + ph < ty + th) num7++;     // 上高
135	        if (num7 === 2) continue;
136	      }
137	      return { type: t, x: i, y: j };
138	    }
139	  }
140	  return null;
141	}
142

## E. 明确未实现 / 占位的功能 Tile（在 Game/Wiring 中找不到处理或只有占位）

**纯占位（devices 只 toast/sfx）：**
- **35 南瓜灯**、**139 音乐盒**、**207 水喷泉**、**410 天塔柱（月柱）**、**480 血月柱**、**509 虚空柱** — toast"装置切换(音乐/喷泉/天塔柱系统未接入)"（devices.ts:326）
- **455 派对中心**（派对事件未接）、**425 广播盒**（无木牌/聊天广播）
- **216 烟花火箭 / 335 烟花盒 / 338 烟花喷泉** — 只有 roar 音效，无真实烟花投射物
- **244 泡泡机 / 565 造雾机 / 593/594 火山** — 仅翻帧（+火山音效），无粒子效果系统
- **15/497 马桶** — 仅溅水声

**有翻帧但核心功能缺失：**
- **421/422 传送带**：旋向互换已实现，**实体输送（推动玩家/NPC/掉落物）未实现**（`Enemy.ts:1163` 注释明言 ConveyorBelt 特例不移植）
- **506 巴斯特雕像**：翻帧有，**+5 防御光环未接 BuffState**
- **372 和平蜡烛**：可亮灭，**刷怪率抑制未实现**（VanillaSpawner 只读水蜡烛）
- **314 矿车轨道压板**：无矿车/乘骑系统
- **476 高尔夫球洞 / 494 高尔夫球座**：无高尔夫系统
- **209 大炮**：弹道有，但**弹药（cannonball）未接入，用巨石投射物近似**

**注册了 tile 但完全无功能处理：**
- **219 / 642 提取机（Extractinator/叶绿提取机）**：仅 CaveHousePass 世界生成放置（CaveHousePass.ts:707），**silt/slush/沙漠化石投入转化未实现**（silt 只会下落）
- **395 物品展示框 / 334 旧武器架 / 2699 武器架 / 470 模特 / 128 旧模特 / 471-473（DisplayDoll/HatRack 类）**：仅注册与破坏回收 `place_` 物品，**无存放/展示 UI**
- **125 水晶球**：**Clairvoyance buff 未接**
- **13 放置瓶族（含瓶中星 StarinaBottle）**：**魔力再生光环未实现**（全 src 仅 i18n 名字）
- **27 向日葵**：仅 SceneMetrics 计数（-10 腐化/猩红、墓园减半），**+2 移速光环未实现**
- **138 巨石 tile**：仅世界生成（TrapsPass.ts:15），**失支撑滚动/砸人未实现**（巨石投射物只来自 531 雕像与 209 大炮近似）
- **210 地雷**：只有电路引爆，**无 NPC/玩家踩踏物理触发**（原版 LandmineCheck 语义）
- **237 蜥蜴祭坛**：只在致动保护名单（Wiring.ts:588），**右键召石巨人均未实现**
- **墓地墓碑 85 族**：墓园视觉强度有（Game.ts:3487），但**鬼魂刷怪/墓园 NPC 未确认**
- **蜂蜜瀑布 345 / 各 liquid-fall 块**：渲染注册，无液体关联逻辑

**其它说明：** 宝石锁 440 的"钥匙开锁"侧（锁定/解锁状态机）未见实现，目前只有 3×3 Trip；测重板 428 的"物品称重"未实现（只按玩家 AABB）。
